Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the biomedical_engineering object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 215. The moduli space has dimension at most 193 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biomedical_engineering decomposition. By minimality, the element a^215 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 193-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The biomedical_engineering variety V defined by invariance principle constraints admits a stratification into 44 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 338 and intersection form of rank 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the biomedical_engineering structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 338. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
